### mb-0009

Arguments: `mailbox-case`

stdout:

```diff
- MBX-MQ-01 default queue is FIFO and counts -> new: n=0 has=false | enqueue n=1 has=true n=2 has=true n=3 has=true | dequeue [a, b, c] then null | after n=0 has=false
+ MBX-MQ-01 default queue is FIFO and counts -> new: n=0 has=false | enqueue n=1 has=true n=2 has=true n=3 has=true | dequeue [c, b, a] then null | after n=0 has=false
```

### mb-0010

Arguments: `mailbox-case`

stdout:

```diff
- MBX-MQ-02 default queue stays FIFO when enqueue and dequeue interleave -> first=a rest=[b, c]
+ MBX-MQ-02 default queue stays FIFO when enqueue and dequeue interleave -> first=b rest=[c, a]
```
